- **pathway-functional-analysis-workflow**: Use when you have an LC-MS metabolomics feature list (m/z, optionally p-values/fold changes) and want biological interpretation without prior identification — feature preparation, mummichog functional analysis from m/z, pathway/enrichment analysis, and pathway-level interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/workflows/pathway-functional-analysis) · 2 copies)
- **pathway-gemini-review-prep**: Use when preparing safe read-only Gemini CLI review prompts and checklists. ([zahin-abrar/pathway-converter](https://github.com/zahin-abrar/pathway-converter/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/pathway-gemini-review-prep))
- **pathway-gene-matrix-filtering**: Use when initializing p-scETM with trainable_gene_emb_dim set to zero, or whenever a known gene-set matrix must be size-filtered before being used as a fixed gene embedding in topic models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pathway-gene-matrix-filtering))
- **pathway-gene-set-mapping-kegg-biocarta**: Use when a genes-by-samples expression matrix (e.g., microarray) must be decomposed into predefined KEGG/Biocarta pathway gene sets (~400 pathways and complexes) as input to SVD-based pathway activity analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-gene-set-mapping-kegg-biocarta))
- **pathway-geneset-curation-kegg-biocarta**: Use when a gene expression analysis needs a predefined gene-set universe (KEGG, Biocarta pathways and complexes) to quantify pathway activity or compute enrichment scores from gene rankings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-geneset-curation-kegg-biocarta))
- **pathway-geneset-formatting**: Use when preparing pathway gene sets (MSigDB collections, csv, or gmt files) as input to SCPA or another pathway-analysis method that expects a structured pathway table mapping pathway names to gene sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pathway-geneset-formatting))
- **pathway-geneset-mapping**: Use when you have a time-series expression matrix, known TF–gene interactions, and pathway gene sets (KEGG/Reactome/GO), and you need to determine which pathways are significantly regulated by TFs activated or repressed at specific time points. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pathway-geneset-mapping))
- **pathway-glossary**: Generate glossary terms for an Alan Hirsch pathway page. Produces 5–10 movemental vocabulary definitions drawn from Alan's books, using his language, linked to the full glossary. These are the terms a reader needs to inhabit this pathway — not a dictionary, but a language school. ([JoshuaShepherd/my-skills](https://github.com/JoshuaShepherd/my-skills/tree/HEAD/repo-specific/alan-hirsch/pathway-glossary) · ★3)
- **pathway-gmt-gene-module-loading**: Use when a single-cell dataset will be analyzed with VEGA or another annotation-informed latent variable model and a GMT file of gene module membership (Reactome, MSigDB, GO, PANTHER) must be loaded to define the decoder wiring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pathway-gmt-gene-module-loading))
- **pathway-go-enrichment**: Gene Ontology over-representation analysis using clusterProfiler enrichGO. Use when identifying biological functions enriched in a gene list from differential expression or other analyses. Supports all three ontologies (BP, MF, CC), multiple ID types, and customizable statistical thresholds. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/pathway-analysis-go-enrichment) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **pathway-go-enrichment**: Find enriched Gene Ontology (GO) terms for a user-provided gene list. Takes a list of genes stored in adata.uns['go_genes'] and tests which GO biological processes, molecular functions, or cellular components are over-represented. Uses Fisher's exact test via gseapy with Benjamini-Hochberg FDR correction. Requires species specification (human or mouse). ([chenyhvvvv/STAT-agent](https://github.com/chenyhvvvv/STAT-agent/tree/HEAD/src/stat_agent/skills/pathway-GO-enrichment) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **pathway-graph-kernel-smoothing**: Use when metabolites map onto a pathway graph (e.g., KEGG edges from the KEGG reaction database) that is sparse or disconnected, and you want to incorporate that graph topology into a kernel machine association test via a regularized normalized Laplacian. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-graph-kernel-smoothing))
- **pathway-graph-simulation-edge-permutation**: Use when you need to evaluate robustness or statistical power of pathway-structured association tests (e.g. kernel machine tests on Laplacian-derived pathway kernels) under controlled amounts of incorrect or incomplete pathway graph knowledge. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-graph-simulation-edge-permutation))
- **pathway-graph-topology-analysis**: Use when testing phenotype–pathway associations from metabolomics data where metabolites can be mapped to a pathway graph (e.g., KEGG reaction edges) and the graph's topography should inform the kernel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-graph-topology-analysis))
- **pathway-gsea**: Gene Set Enrichment Analysis using clusterProfiler gseGO and gseKEGG. Use when analyzing ranked gene lists to find coordinated expression changes in gene sets without arbitrary significance cutoffs. Detects subtle but coordinated expression changes. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/pathway-analysis-gsea) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **pathway-hyperlink-classification-training**: Use when labeled pathway sets (positives from TomatoCyc, negatives from MetaCyc plus random sampling) must be classified from metabolite correlation network waveforms, or when reproducing NetAurHPD-style AUC benchmarks. - **pathway-impact-plot-visualization**: Use when you have ORA enrichment results (adjusted Fisher's exact-test p-values on KEGG pathways) plus a significant metabolite list, and need to prioritize pathways by network topology impact rather than p-value rank alone. - **pathway-importance-scoring**: Use when you have fitted a pathway-level multi-omics model (PathIntegrate MB-PLS/Single-View or DIABLO on ssPA-transformed data) and need ranked pathway importance statistics with permutation-tested significance or detection AUCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-importance-scoring))
- **pathway-inference-agent**: Use this agent when you need to create a pathway summary for a gene after completing the main gene review. - **pathway-kegg-pathways**: KEGG pathway and module enrichment analysis using clusterProfiler enrichKEGG and enrichMKEGG. - **pathway-kernel-association-testing**: Use when you have paired genotype and phenotype data and want to test for association between a predefined genetic pathway (set of genes or variants) and a quantitative or binary trait, particularly when individual-variant tests lack power or when pathway-level aggregation is scientifically. - **pathway-level-activity-scoring**: Use when you have a metabolite intensity matrix (samples × metabolites) with assigned annotations (peak IDs mapped to KEGG or ChEBI compound IDs), a metabolic pathway database, and need to rank pathways by activity level within experimental groups or comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pathway-level-activity-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **pathway-level-clustering**: Use when you have a PDS score matrix (0–1 pathway deregulation scores) restricted to Lilikoi-selected pathway features and want to check whether pathway-level profiles visually separate phenotype classes before or after classification modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-level-clustering))

- **pathway-level-sample-stratification**: Use when you have per-sample metabolite abundance data and a metabolite-to-pathway association table, and need to assign each sample a dysregulation score for each pathway to enable downstream pathway-level classification, feature selection, or survival analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pathway-level-sample-stratification))
- **pathway-level-statistical-inference**: Use when when you have genotype data (variants grouped by pathway annotation) and quantitative or binary phenotype data, and you want to test whether a pathway as a whole is associated with the phenotype, rather than testing individual variants. - **pathway-level-vs-molecular-level-signal-detection-benchmarking**: Use when you need to empirically justify a pathway-transformation step by comparing pathway-level versus molecular-level univariate detection power on injected-signal simulations spanning effect sizes 0–1.
